[GAME] Welcome to Secret Mafia! You are Player 0.
Your role: Villager
Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

[GAME] No one was killed tonight.
[GAME] Day breaks. Discuss for 3 rounds, then a vote will follow.

[COMPLETION]
**Player 0 (Villager):** Alright, everyone, let's start discussing who we think might be the Mafia. I don't have any special abilities, but I'm here to help figure out who the real threat is. Has anyone noticed anything suspicious about any of the players? I'm just a regular villager, so I'm going to listen carefully to what others say and try to piece together any clues. Let's keep the conversation going and try to eliminate the Mafia together. Who do you all think is the biggest suspect?

[ACTION=**Player 0 (Villager):** Alright, everyone, let's start discussing who we think might be the Mafia. I don't have any special abilities, but I'm here to help figure out who the real threat is. [GAME] Welcome to Secret Mafia! You are Player 4.
Your role: Villager
Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

Your role: Villager
Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).
